# Аудит 14.09.2026 — НАХОДКИ НЕ ПРОВЕРЕНЫ

Обход всех подсистем закончился, а проверка скептиками — нет: её остановили.
Значит, **ни одна строчка ниже не подтверждена**. Сегодня скептики уже отбрасывали
мои собственные уверенные выводы, и два моих утверждения оказались неверными, так что
читать это надо как список мест, куда стоит посмотреть, а не как список дефектов.

Сводка: 347 различных находок, из них 31 critical и 115 high.
В четверг: прогнать проверку, и только то, что её переживёт, чинить.

### [auth] Refund retry mints a fresh idempotency key, so a dropped response lets the same refund leave Montonio twice
- вид: money · уверенность автора: likely
- механизм: The route's only double-refund guard is a check-then-act: it reads the order, computes `left = refundableAmount(...)` and refuses when `left <= 0` (route.ts:125-127). That guard only holds once the first refund has been *recorded* in Postgres. The provider call itself is sent with `idempotencyKey: randomUUID()` (route.ts:191) — a new key on every attempt — so Montonio has nothing to deduplicate ag
- как проявится: Renat refunds 49 € on order R-1043 from his phone in the salon. Montonio accepts the refund; the Wi-Fi drops (or the Vercel function hits its wall) before the JSON answer gets back. - где смотреть: src/app/api/admin/orders/[id]/refund/route.ts:191 — `idempotencyKey: randomUUID(),` with the comment asserting it prevents a double send · src/app/api/admin/orders/[id]/refund/route.ts:125-127 — `const left = refundableAmount(value, order.payment); if (!(left > 0)) return bad("already_refunded"...)` is the only dedupe, and it reads state the first attempt may not have written yet · src/app/api/adm

- вид: fragility · уверенность автора: certain

### [auth] verifyPassword() accepts ANY password when the stored hash's key segment decodes to zero bytes
- вид: privacy · уверенность автора: certain
- механизм: verifyPassword derives the scrypt output length from the stored key itself — `scryptSync(password, salt, want.length, …)` — and then compares. If parts[5] (the key half of `scrypt$N$r$p$salt$key`) decodes to an empty Buffer, want.length is 0, scryptSync returns a zero-length Buffer, and `want.length === got.length && timingSafeEqual(want, got)` is `true` for every password. Buffer.from(x,"base64")
- как проявится: ADMIN_PASSWORD_HASH in the Vercel project is set to a value whose final `$`-segment is empty or non-base64 — a copy-paste that clipped the last field, or a re-paste after an edit. Nothing looks broken: /api/admin/me still answers `configured: true` and the login card renders normally. Anyone who POS
- где смотреть: src/lib/auth.ts:148 — `const want = Buffer.from(parts[5], "base64");` lenient decode, no length check · src/lib/auth.ts:150 — `scryptSync(password, salt, want.length, …)` keylen taken from the stored key · src/lib/auth.ts:151 — `return want.length === got.length && timingSafeEqual(want, got);` 0===0 and timingSafeEqual(empty,empty) is true · src/lib/auth.ts:138 — the doc comment claims "False if u

- как проявится: Renat cancels R-100042 in the panel; the customer gets «Заказ отменён» and the goods go back on the shelf. Hours later Montonio abandons the unfinished payment and posts the webhook; the order flips to «не оплачен». Three days later the cron writes to the same customer: «Заказ ждёт оплаты, осталось 
- где смотреть: src/lib/payments/apply.ts:538-541 — the failed branch has no guard for `cancelled` · src/lib/payments/montonio.ts:104-108 — ABANDONED/CANCELLED map to `failed` · src/lib/flows.ts:946 — the unpaid loop selects status in ('new','failed') · src/lib/flows.ts:972-977 — the reminder's pay-again URL, `s=failed&o=<id>`

### [payments] A cancelled order can be reopened — or quietly paid — by a late provider token
- вид: correctness · уверенность автора: likely
- механизм: `applyPaymentResult` only treats `paid` as terminal (apply.ts:386-395). For any other status it applies the result to whatever state the order is in: a `failed` token sets the order to `failed` if it is not already (apply.ts:538-541) and a `paid` token settles it in full (apply.ts:517-536). Neither checks for `cancelled`. POST /api/payments/create/ does treat cancelled as terminal (`order_closed`,
- как проявится: Renat cancels an unpaid order by hand because the customer phoned to say he changed his mind; the customer gets the «Заказ отменён» letter. The bank link the customer had opened earlier completes anyway, or Montonio redelivers its ABANDONED token. In the first case the cancelled order becomes `paid`
- где смотреть: src/lib/payments/apply.ts:386-395 — alreadyPaid() is the only terminal state; `cancelled` and `refunded` are not in it (a refunded order is saved only incidentally, by payment.status still reading "paid") · src/lib/payments/apply.ts:538-541 — `if (order.status !== "failed") setOrderStatus(order.id, "failed", …)` — from `cancelled` too · src/lib/payments/apply.ts:517-536 — a paid token settles a ca

### [payments] The refund webhook's over-refund clamp falls back to the unclamped amount exactly when it bites, and is measured against the wrong total
- вид: correctness · уверенность автора: certain
- механизм: notify/route.ts:171 clamps the reported refund to what is left, then :176 writes `amount > 0 ? amount : note.amount` — so when the remainder is 0 (the case the clamp exists for) the full unclamped figure is recorded instead. The clamp is also computed from `Number(order.total)` rather than `refundValue(order)` (total + what a gift card paid), which is the figure every other refund calculation uses
- как проявится: An order of 40 € is fully refunded and recorded. Renat, thinking it did not go through, issues a second refund of 40 € in Montonio's portal. It arrives with a new refundUuid, so foldRefund does not merge it; refundableAmount is 0, the ternary discards the clamp, and 40 € is added on top. The order c
- где смотреть: src/app/api/payments/notify/route.ts:171 — `const amount = Math.min(note.amount, refundableAmount(Number(order.total) || 0, order.payment));` · src/app/api/payments/notify/route.ts:176 — `amount: amount > 0 ? amount : note.amount` — the clamp is discarded at 0 · src/app/api/payments/notify/route.ts:165-169 — the comment claims "the ledger can never say more went back than the order was worth" · sr

- где смотреть: src/app/api/account/code/route.ts:76 — `rateLimit("account-code-ip", ip, 3, WINDOW_MS) || rateLimit("account-code-mail", email, 3, WINDOW_MS)` · src/app/api/account/code/route.ts:40 — `const WINDOW_MS = 15 * 60 * 1000;` · src/lib/customers.ts:34 — `CODE_MAX_ATTEMPTS = 5` · src/lib/customers.ts:276-279 — the fifth failure kills the code, forcing a fresh request

- как проявится: A crawler or a burst of real traffic touches /api/track and /api/giftcards/check from more than 5000 distinct addresses inside one hour on a single warm Vercel instance. ### [customers] The redeem ceiling rounds up, so a basket can pay more of itself in points than the stated redeemMaxPct
- вид: correctness · уверенность автора: certain
- механизм: The cap is `eurosToPoints(subtotal * redeemMaxPct / 100)`, and eurosToPoints rounds to the nearest whole point rather than flooring. The browser's preview mirrors it exactly (Math.round of the same expression), so client and server agree — they are both above the limit the cabinet quotes. Bounded at half a euro and still floored by what is left to pay, so it can never make an order free, but it is
- как проявится: redeemMaxPct is the default 30 and a basket comes to 11.70 €. 30 % is 3.51, which rounds to 4 points, so 4.00 € — 34 % of the basket — is taken off, against a cabinet that promises no more than 30 %.
- где смотреть: src/lib/loyalty.ts:402-403 — `const capFromSubtotal = eurosToPoints((Math.max(0, num(subtotal)) * settings.redeemMaxPct) / 100)` · src/lib/loyalty.ts:199-202 — `eurosToPoints` = `Math.round(cents / 100)` · public/shop2/app.js:7984 — `var cap = Math.round(cartSum() * st.redeemMaxPct / 100)` · public/shop2/app.js:12776 — «Списать можно при оформлении — не больше N% от суммы корзины»

- invoices: The checkout cannot be talked into an invoice it should not get, from a stale tab or a doctored body: the method, the country (invoice_country, src/lib/orders.ts:1285), the zero total (invoice_zero_total, :1341

- giftcards: normaliseCode() refuses any character outside the alphabet instead of correcting it, so a typo can never land on someone else's card (giftcards.ts:203-212). One edge I did trace and it is genuinely broken, tho
